### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 17 日

1 群益投顧 
華邦電 (2344 TT) 宣 布 將 以 全 現 金 11.2 億美元 方 式 取 得 Infineon (IFX  DE )的 NOR Flash
與 F-RAM 事業 部 100% 股權 ， 本 交 易 預 計 於 2H27 完成 。 此 次 收 購 對 華 邦 電 而 言 ， 可
藉此取得 較 完整 NOR Flash 線 ， 並 新 增 F-RAM 產品與相關全球客戶、研發資源，進
一步提高在 NOR Flash 市 占 率。目 前 記 憶 體 報 價 上 漲 有 利華邦電 短 期 獲 利 增 加，矽電
容 則為新 獲 利 來 源 ， 中 長 期 則 可 望 受 惠Spansion 收 購 效 益 ， 因 此 對 華 邦 電 建 議Buy 。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw  
華 邦 電 宣 布 將 以 全 現 金 方 式 取 得 Infineon 的 NOR Flash 與 F-RAM 事業
100% 的股權 ：  
09/16 /2026 華 邦 電 發 布 重 訊，宣 布 將與 德 國 半 導 體 大 廠 Infineon (Infineon )旗
下的 Infineon Technologies LLC 簽 署 股 份 購 買 協 議 。華 邦 電 將 以 全 現 金 約
11.2 億 美 元 ( 以 匯 率 31.6 估 算 約 新 台 幣 353.92 億元 ) ， 取 得 Infineon 
Technologies LLC 旗下 NOR Flash 與 F-RAM 事 業 經 重 組 後 標 的 公 司 的 100%
股權。  
交 易 完 成 後 ， 華 邦 電 將 直 接 或 間 接 持 有 標 的 公 司100% 股 權 ， 標 的 公 司 並 將
重 新 以 「Spansion 」 為 名 營 運 ， 形 成Winbond 與 Spansion 雙 品 牌 布 局 。Spansion
總 部 續 留 美 國 加 州 聖 荷 西(San Jose )，保 留 原 Infineon /Cypress 的 研 發 核 心 與
歐 美 技 術 團 隊(跨 十 餘 國 人 才)。  
交 易 總 金 額 將 依 Stock Purchase Agreement 所 約 定 之 交 割 日 財 務 狀 況 及 價 格
調 整 機 制 結 算 後 確 定，資 金 來 源 為 自 有 資 金 或 融 資 活 動 支 應。本 交 易 預 計 於
2H27 完 成 ， 惟 仍 須 取 得 相 關 主 管 機 關 核 准 ， 並 達 成 或 依 法 豁 免 股 份 購 買 協
議 所 約 定 之 其 他 交 割 先 決 條 件 。 
華 邦 電 透 過 此 交 易擴充 高階 NOR Flash 和 新 增 F-RAM 產 品 線 ， 市 占 穩 居
NOR Flash 第一 ：  
此次收購 對 華 邦 電 而 言 ， 則 可 藉 此 取 得 完 整 NOR Flash 線 ， 並 新 增 F-RAM
產 品 與 相 關 全 球 客 戶、 研 發 資 源，進 一 步 提 高在 NOR Flash 市 占 率 。依 Omdia
統 計 ，2025 年 全 球 NOR Flash 市 場 規 模 約 34 億 美 元 ， 其 中 華 邦 電 市 占23%
居 首 ， 其 次 為 兆 易 創 新(603986 SHA) 及旺宏 (2337 TT) ， 各 占 17% ， Infineon
占 11% 、 排 名 第 四 。 
從 產 品 及 客 戶 結 構 來 看，雙 方 既 有 客 戶 重 疊 程 度 不 高。華 邦 電 NOR Flash 應
用以 PC 、 NB 市 場 為 主 ，Infineon 相 關 業 務 則 具 有 較 多 歐 美 級 車 用 客 戶 ， 有
助 公 司 收 購 後 直 接 擴 大 NOR Flash 規 模 ， 並 補 足 不 同 區 域 及 終 端 市 場 的 客
戶布局。  
此 外 ， 此 次 交 易 另 一 重 點 在 研 發 團 隊 。 收 購 標 的 具 有 逾30 年 發 展 歷 史 ， 研
發 據 點 分 布 歐 洲 、 亞 洲 、 東 南 亞 及 美 國 ， 其 中 以 美 西 團 隊 規 模 較 大。 標的公
司 Spansion 已 在 車 用 及 工 控 記 憶 體 市 場 累 積 長 期 產 品 與 市 場 經 驗 ， 收 購 後
可望使華邦電直接取得既有產品及市場布局，降低進入高階車用記憶體市
場 從 零 建 立 產 品 與 客 戶 關 係 的 時 間 。 不 過 ， 若 後 續 涉 及 製 程 或 生 產 地 調 整 ，
仍 須 依 客 戶 要 求 進 行 PCN 及 相 關 驗 證 ， 因 此 客 戶 及 產 品 整 合 效 益 仍 需 時 間
逐 步 實 現 。 
Infineon 著重 資 產 組 合 調 整 ，出售 NOR Flash 事業， 而 非 對 NOR Flash 需
求 前 景 的 否 定：

產業速報

2 群益投顧 
本 次 交 易 較 偏 向 Infineon 的 資 產 組 合 調 整 ， 而 非 對 NOR Flash 需 求 前 景 的
否定， Infineon 自 己 目 前 仍 是 NOR Flash 第四大廠， 2025 年 市 占 約 11.5% ，
並 不 是 競 爭 力 不 佳 才 處 份 。 
Infineon 仍 保 留 多 項 特 殊 記 憶 體 產 品 ， 並 沒 有 全 面 退 出 記 憶 體 ， 公 司 特 別 說
明 交 易 完 成 後 仍 會 保 留 SRAM、HYPERRAM 、nvSRAM 及 輻 射 強 化 型 SONOS
記 憶 體 等 特 殊 記 憶 體 產 品 。 
Infineon 將 資 本 配 置 進 一 步 集 中 在 核 心 成 長 領 域 ， 重 點 包 括AI 資 料 中 心 電
源 、 車 用 功 率 半 導 體 、MCU 及 連 接 等 ； 公 司 2026 財 年 也 大 幅 提 高 AI 電源
相 關 營 收 目 標 。 
中 長 期 可 改 善 生 產 成 本：  
原 Infineon 記 憶 體 產 品 目 前 仍 有 相 當 比 例 採外 部 晶 圓 代 工 ， 華 邦 電 未 來 可
評 估 利 用 自 身 12 吋 晶 圓 產 能 進 行 部 分 產 品 內 製 ， 理 論 上 可 提 高 自 有 產 能 利
用 率 並 降 低 外 部 製 造 成 本。不 過，車 用 產 品 對 供 應 商 及 製 造 流 程 變 更 具 有 較
高 要 求 ， 若 涉 及 晶 圓 製 造 地 或 製 程 轉 換 ， 仍 須 經 過PCN 及 重 新 驗 證 ， 因 此
相 關 成 本 綜 效 屬 中 長 期 效 益 。 
預期 256Mb 及 以 上 高 容 量 產 品 在 2H26 平 均 價 格 仍 有 90 -110% 的 上 漲 空 間： 
根據 TrendForce 報 告 ， NOR Flash 市 場 正 經 歷 近 10 年 來 最 重 要 的 供 需 結 構
轉變； AI 需 求 排 擠 ， 供 應 商 NOR Flash 位 元 產 出 增 加 有 限 ， 加 上 高 階 應 用
需 求 升 級 ， 預 期 2H26 供 需 仍 舊 失 衡 ， 整 體 合 約 價 格 維 持 在 高 點 ， 高 容 量 產
品 價 格 甚 至 可 能 翻 倍 成 長 。 
供 給 端 方 面，即 便 台 系、陸 系 供 應 商 皆 已 提 高 資 本 支 出 並 擴 產，但 因 新 增 產
能 必 須 經 過 製 程 微 縮、良 率 爬 坡、產 品 驗 證，以 及 納 入 客 戶 規 格 與 系 統 設 計
(design -in) ， 無 法 立 即 滿 足 市 場 的 需 求 ， 因 此 ， 短 期 內 中 高 容 量NOR Flash
市 場 的 缺 口 仍 無 法 獲 得 滿 足 。 
NOR Flash 需 求 方 面 ， 除 傳 統 的 先 進 駕 駛 輔 助 系 統(ADAS) 、 數 位 座 艙 及 工
控 應 用 ，AI 與 航 太 通 訊 領 域 則 為 2026 年 新 增 需 求 。 以 AI 伺 服 器 為 例 ， 單
台對 NOR 的 需 求 量 為 傳 統 伺 服 器 的 3~5 倍 ， 且 AI 伺 服 器 內 部 配 備 大 量
Retimer 晶片，每顆 Retimer 均需要獨立 NOR Flash 載 入 微 碼 ， 推 升 高 容 量
及 Octal SPI NOR 需 求 。 此 外 ，Edge -AI 、 AI PC 與 機 器 人 等 本 地 推 論 設 備 帶
動 模 型 韌 體 規 模 數 倍 膨 脹，也 提 升 高 密 度 NOR Flash 需 求。低 軌 衛 星 與 航 太
電 子 領 域 方 面 ， 衛 星 本 體 的 核 心 晶 片 組 需 要 可 靠 度 高 的 NOR Flash 執行快
速 開 機 與 韌 體 備 份 ， 地 面 接 收 天 線 亦 內 建 NOR 以 維 持 即 時 運 算 與 OTA 韌
體 更 新 。 而 航 太 用 NOR 要 求 具 備 抗 輻 射 與 耐 極 端 溫 差 特 性 ， 為 台 系 、 美 系
廠 商 建 立 技 術 壁 壘 。 
TrendForce 表 示 ， 1H26 NOR Flash 合 約 價 平 均 累 積 漲 幅 達 100 -120% ，預期
2H26 不 同 容 量 產 品 價 格 走 勢 將 分 化 。256Mb 及 以 上 高 容 量 產 品 有 AI server 、
車 用 等 強 勁 需 求 ， 加 上 供 給 主 力 台 系 業 者 的 新 增 位 元 有 限 ， 預 估2H26 平均
價格仍有 90 -110% 的 上 漲 空 間 。 而 128Mb 及 以 下 的 中 低 容 量 市 場 ， 隨 著 中
系 供 應 商 產 能 逐 步 釋 出 ， 消 費 類 產 品 價 格 漲 勢 趨 緩 ， 後 續 將 進 入 高 檔 盤 整 ，
預估 4Q26 漲 幅 落 在 10 -20% 之 間 。

產業速報

3 群益投顧 
華 邦 電 資 本 配 置 和 回 收 期 為 未來 觀 察 重 點 ：  
本 次 交 易 金 額 達 11.2 億美元 (約 353.92 億元 )， 交 易 標 的 為 重 組 後NOR Flash
及 F-RAM 事 業 之 100% 股 權 ， 並 不 包 含 晶 圓 廠 等 大 規 模 製 造 資 產 ， 因 此 以
交 易 金 額 觀 察 ， 價 格 並 不 低 。 
收 購 交 易 金 額 約 353.92 億 元 ， 資 金 將 以 自 有 資 金 或 融 資 活 動 支 應 。 另 一 方
面，公 司 另 規 劃 發 行 第 四 次 及 第 五 次 無 擔 保 轉 換 公 司 債，發 行 面 額 上 限 分 別
為 140 億元及 40 億 元 ， 合 計 最 高 180 億 元 ， 票 面 利 率 均 為 0% ，募得資金
主 要 用 於 產 能 擴 充、先 進 製 程 研 發 及 機 器 設 備 購 置。形 成 外 部 取 得 產 品 與 市
場、內 部 擴 充 製 造 能 力 的 資 本 配 置 方 向。惟 兩 項 計 畫 同 步 推 進，未 來 資 金 需
求 及 資 本 支 出 均 將 增 加，仍 需 觀 察 收 購 資 金 來 源、 融 資 成 本 及 CB 未 來 轉 換
所 可 能 產 生 的 股 本 稀 釋 效 果 。 
這次交易 華 邦 電 主 要 取 得 產 品 、 技 術 、IP 、 客 戶 及 全 球 營 運 資 源 ， 後 續 仍 需
透 過 產 品 擴 張、客 戶 深 化 及 製 造 資 源 整 合 逐 步 創 造 綜 效；若 實 際 獲 利 貢 獻 低
於 預 期 ， 則 可 能 使 投 資 回 收 期 拉 長 ， 並 增 加 併 購 資 本 配 置 的 壓 力 。 
華 邦 電 建 議 Buy ：  
AI 的 發 展 改 變 產 業 追 求 性 價 比 與 記 憶 體 單 價 的 傳 統 ， 轉 而 重 視 效 能 、 算 力
及 大 容 量 。 記 憶 體 市 場 供 需 出 現 結 構 性 改 變 。 進 入3Q26 DRAM 與 Flash 的
價 格 仍 維 持 上 行 趨 勢，雖 然 價 格 上 漲 趨 勢 較 緩，但 毛 利 率 仍 持 續 向 上。預 估
華邦電 2026 年 稅 後 EPS 為 24.85 元 (不 含 此 次 收 購)。除 AI 伺 服 器 需 求 外 ，
交換器及 Edge AI 應 用 亦 帶 動 NOR Flash 與 SLC NAND 需 求 持 續 成 長 ， 華
邦電憑藉 NOR Flash 全 球 市 占 率 第 一 及 SLC NAND 擴 產 ， 可 望 持 續 受 惠 。
此 外 ， 公 司 矽 電 容 目 前 產 能 接 近 滿 載 ， 受 惠AI 先 進 封 裝 需 求 快 速 成 長 ， 將
成 為 新 的 獲 利 來 源， 中長期 則 可 望 受惠 Spansion 收購 效益 ， 因此 對 華 邦 電
建議 Buy 。

### Page 4

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 17 日

4 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2368金像電 
(Gold Circuit Electronics)

報 告 日 期 ：2026/09/22 研 究 員： 方姿婷  (Lesley  Fang ) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
重點摘要：  
⚫ 2Q26 營收 符 合 預 期，毛 利 率 受人 民 幣 升 值 匯 率 干 擾、直 接 人 工
成 本 增 加 影 響，毛 利 率 略 低 於35%，展 望 3Q26 毛 利 率 應 可 回 歸
常態增至 37~38% 之間 。 
⚫ AI 伺 服 器 帶 動 高 階 銅 箔 基 板與 PCB(HDI 、HLC) 厚板 需 求 強 勁，
2H26 ASIC 客戶 (AWS Trainium 4 及 Google TPU V9) 等，預計採
用 HDI 與 HLC 混 壓 設 計 ， 金 像 電 於3~4 階 HDI 混壓技術已經
相 對 純 熟 可 順 利 供 應 。 
⚫ 2H26 至 2027 年， CCL /PCB 產 業 升 級 朝 向1.6T Switch 發 展 ， 預
期 板 層 數 提 升 至44~52L 、CCL 材 料 端 為 M8 或 M9 等 級 供 應 ，
對 於 金 像 電 而 言 ，PCB 板 層 數 提 升 幅 度 達15~20% 。

一. 2Q26 營收與財報 符合市場預期， 3Q26 展望正向  
金像電 2Q26 營收 242.79 億元， +25.71%QoQ ，+75.26%YoY ， 毛 利
率 34.65% ，稅 後 淨 利 47.83 億 元，稅 後 EPS+9.24 元。對於 2H26 看
法，因 應 客 戶 強 勁 需 求(AI ASIC 厚板 )，產 能 擴 充 方 面，新 增 蘇 州、
泰國新廠 HDI 厚 板 支 應。展望 3Q26 ，累計 7~8 月 份 金 像 電 營 收 達
成率 67% 符 合 市 場 預 期 ， 預 估 金 像 電 營 收 為 305.86 億 元 ，
+25.98%QoQ ， 毛 利 率 37.55% ， 稅 後 淨 利 67.04 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後
EPS+12.72 元。

二. AI 伺服器帶動 CCL/PCB 產值提升，產能擴充 積極  
宏遠投顧觀察 ，AI 伺 服 器 與 先 進 封 裝 等 需 求 日 增 ，同 時 對 於 一 般
型 伺 服 器 用 板、高 階Switch 用 板、AI 伺 服 器 用 板，需 求 迅 速 拉 升。
回顧近年來 ，金 像 電 產 能 擴 充，各 廠 區 台 灣 廠30 億 元、蘇 州 廠 38
億 元、常 熟 一 廠 10 億 元、常 熟 二 廠 3 億 元，2H26 ASIC 客戶 (AWS 
Trainium 4 及 Google TPU V9) 等，預 計 採 用 HDI 與 HLC 混 壓 設 計，
金像電於 3~4 階 HDI 混 壓 技 術 已 經 相 對 純 熟 ， 對 於2H26~2027 年
未 來 訂 單 能 見 度 影 響 不 大， 未 來 出 貨 將 可 順 利 供 貨。2027 年隨著
NVIDIA  Rubin 平 台 將 帶 動CCL M8+ 、M9 材 料 出 貨 攀 升 ，AI ASIC 
CCL 拉 貨 動 能呈現 50~60% 高 度 成 長 ，CCL 、PCB 產 品 組 合 持 續 優
化。2027 年 起，CCL 與 PCB 產 業 產 品 升 級，除一般型伺服器 Intel 
Diamond Rapids 平 台 、AMD Zen6 Venice 平 台 採 用PCIe Gen6 與 Ultra 
Low Loss CCL(M7) ，PCB 板層數增至 20~24L ；2027 年 ， 隨 著1.6T 
投資建議：買進 
目標價位：1,290.00 元 
      16.00X 2027 EPS

Switch 逐步導入 M8/M9 CCL(HLC 層數 46~52L 、 玻 纖 Low Dk2/Q
布、銅箔 HVLP4/5) ， 預 期 CCL 廠 商 產 品 規 格 顯 著 升 級 。

三. 金像 電對標高階 Switch 板材， 2027 年為成長主軸  
金像電 於 AI 伺 服 器 與新 平 台之 間 設 計 資 料 傳 輸 速 率、資 料 交 換 與
處 理 等 級 提 升 ， 對 於 高 階Switch 網 路 交 換 器 需 求 同 步 增 加 。觀察
AI 產 業 發 展，高 階Switch 於 2026 年 已 全 面 升 級 至800GSwitch ，金
像電 過往 為 品 牌(企業用 Switch )與 白 牌 主 要 交 換 器PCB 供 應 商 ，
以 800G Switch 為 例，PCB 板層數 38~44L 設 計、CCL 材料端為 M8
等 級，金 像 電 營 收 網 通 產 品 已 達10~20% 當 中 多 數 為 高 階Switch 板
材，展 望 2027 年 CCL/PCB 產 業 升 級 朝 向1.6T Switch 發 展，預 期 板
層數提升至 44~52L 、CCL 材 料 端 為 M8 或 M9 等級 供 應 ， 對 於 金
像電而言， PCB 板 層 數 提 升 幅 度 達15~20% 。

四.預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 43.68 /80.57 元 
2026 年受惠 AI 伺 服 器及 ASIC 需 求 持 續 強 勁 的 帶 動 下 ，金像 電為
AI PCB 領 導 廠 商與 AI ASIC 厚 板 主 要 供 甕 商， 將 受 惠AI Server 對
於高 階 厚 板 與HLC 板 材、HDI 板材 強 勁 的 需 求。因而 ，預估 2026
年 金像 電 營 收 為1,086 .36 億元， +81.04%YoY ， 毛 利 率36.75%，稅
後淨利 228 .27 億 元，稅 後 EPS+ 43.68 元。展 望 2027 年，在 AI 伺服
器 需 求 持 續 強 勁 的 帶 動 下 ， 帶 動金像電 PCB 產能 持續 增長， 預估
2027 年 金像 電營收為 1,749 .83 億元， +61.07%YoY ，毛利率為
40.01%， 稅 後 淨 利424.59 億元，稅後 EPS+ 80.57 元。

四.投資建議維持 買進，目標價 1,290 元 
宏 遠 投 顧 認 為，金像 電 營 運 重 點 在 於，營 運 動 能來 自 於一 般 型 伺 服
器 CPU 廠商 Intel、AMD 均 有 新 品 帶 動 新 平 台 升 級 與 相 關 供 應 的 零
組 件 商 機，以 及 攻 入AI 伺 服 器、高 階 網 路 交 換 器與 一 般 型 伺 服 器
主板 規 格 轉 換 成 長 可 期。基 於 上 述，給 予金像 電 投 資 建 議 為 買 進，
目標價 1,290 元(16.00x 20 27 EPS) 。

一. 公司簡介  
金像 電(23 68)為 PCB 廠 商 ，為 全 球 最 大 一 般 型 伺 服 器 PCB 製 造 商 ，
產品應用別 當 中，伺 服 器 與AI 伺 服 器 營 收 比 重 達78% 、網 通 產 品
(包 含 高 階Switch) 達 10~20% 、 其 他 為NB 佔比 5%、 與 其 他 品 項 。

二.2026~2027 年營運概況 及展望  
1.HDI 與 M8 以 上 升 級 仍 為 關 注 焦 點 ，2026~2028 年 產 能 建 置 積 極 
過 往 一 般 型 伺 服 器 用 板 採用 HDI 設計 ， 強 調 細 線 路 設 計 ，CPU 尺
寸變大 Pin C ount 數 量 增 加 至2,000 只~4,000 只 Pin Count ，PCB 板
面積不變之下要走 向 細 線 路化(線寬 /線距 )朝 向 更 細 線 路 設 計 。 製
程上 HDI 經 過 多 次 壓 合 後，PP 在 熱 壓 多 次後，每 一 層 熱 膨 脹 係 數
不同，導致通孔 Crack 或翹曲，製作時容易產生爆板，因此對於
PCB 板廠製作良率會是一大技術難關，而金像電、健鼎等廠商於
高階 HDI 相 對 具 備 生 產 優 勢 與 競 爭 力 ， 相 對 應 台 光 電 、 台 燿 等 高
階 CCL 供 貨 與 升 級 也 成 為 製 程 一 大 瓶 頸 ；回 顧 近 年 來 ， 金 像 電 產
能 擴 充 ， 各 廠 區 台 灣 廠30 億 元 、 蘇 州 廠38 億 元 、 常 熟 一 廠10 億

2481.TT 強茂 
 AI 不泡沫 功率就有夢

[調升評價]   
   公司簡介 
強茂為台灣功率半導體與分離式元件製造大廠，公司採垂直整合佈局，擁有 3 座前段
晶圓製造廠，及 5 座海內外後段封裝測試據點。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 208 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，150 
【大盤指數/股價】45849 / 168.5

➢ 2Q26 獲利符合預期 
強茂 2Q26 營收 39.56 億元，QoQ+15.62%，YoY+16.34%，毛利率
33.22%， 稅後淨利4.88 億元 ，QoQ+69.58%，YoY+93.85%，EPS 1.28
元，獲利大致符合預期。毛利率維持季增趨勢，但營業費用比預期高，
主因是客戶拉貨強勁，備貨及物流相關費用多及提列獎金所致。 
➢ 下半年營收、獲利逐季向上 
AI 伺服器、車用及小訊號產品訂單能見度佳，強茂 B/B ratio 值維持在
1.3~1.5 高檔位置，3Q26 新增部份產能，加上產品調漲，預期營收及毛
利率持續向上；4Q26 有機會切入另一家 CSP 供應鏈，年底增加封測產
能(小訊號為主)、越南 TOREX 預計在 1Q27 交割，整體而言營運動能無
虞。預估強茂 3Q26 營收 42.51 億元，QoQ+7.47%，YoY+30.02%，
毛利率 34.44%，稅後淨利 5.58 億元，QoQ+14.39%，YoY+84.08%，
EPS 1.46 元。 
➢ 歐美重心轉移、非紅供應鏈，台 IDM 廠有訂價權 
在 AI 需求強勁推動下，單機櫃功耗持續上升並導入高壓直流電架構，帶
動功率元件量價同步提升，應用於伺服器功率元件年複合成長率遠高於
產業平均，在產能吃緊情況下，歐美 IDM 廠將重心轉向利潤較佳的高
壓、SiC 以及低壓大電流的 DrMOS 核心市場，排擠中低壓功率元件產
能。同時，安世、揚傑等中資背景企業面臨去中化，伺服器、車用工控
等領域加速將訂單轉向台廠。 
➢ 預估強茂 2027 年 EPS 6.95 元，建議買進，目標價 208 元 
投研部預估強茂 2026 年營收 159.39 億元，YoY+21.73%，稅後淨利
19.21 億元，YoY+61.22%，EPS 5.03 元；預估 2027 年營收 183.38 億
元，YoY+15.05%，稅後淨利 26.57 億元，YoY+38.29%，EPS 6.95 元。
目前強茂 PE 24X ，2026 年最高本益比已超越 2017~2018 年、
2020~2022 年功率元件景氣循環 20X~30X 區間，AI 伺服器的應用有
助拉升功率元件產業及公司評價，給予強茂買進建議，目標價 208 元
(2027F PE 30X)。

2481 強茂 加權指數

### Page 2

- 2 -

個股報告

公司簡介 
強茂股份有限公司成立於 1986 年，為台灣功率半導體與分離式元件製造大廠，公司
採垂直整合(IDM)佈局，擁有 3 座前段晶圓製造廠(4 吋、6 吋、8 吋各一座)，及 5 座
海內外後段封裝測試據點。 
1H26 產品營收比重 ： 金氧半場效電晶體(MOSFET)佔 32%， 蕭特基二極體(Schottky)
佔 23%，整流器(Rectifier)佔 12%，突波抑制器(TVS)佔 9%，靜電防護二極體(ESD 
Diode)佔 7%，齊納二極體(Zener)佔 6%，開關二極體(Switching)佔 6%，雙極性接
面電晶體(BJT)佔 4%，碳化矽(SiC)佔 1%。若以市場應用來看，車用電子佔營收比重
35%為最大，消費型電子佔 20%，電腦運算佔 16%，工控&綠能佔 13%，電源供應
用佔 12%，通訊佔 4%，其中 AI 人工智能應用佔 11%。 
圖 1：強茂產品組合

資料來源：強茂，國票投顧 
2Q26 獲利符合預期 
強茂 2Q26 營收 39.56 億元，QoQ+15.62%，YoY+16.34%，毛利率 33.22%，稅後
淨利 4.88 億元，QoQ+69.58%，YoY+93.85%，EPS 1.28 元，獲利大致符合預期。
毛利率維持季增趨勢，但營業費用比預期高，主因是客戶拉貨強勁，備貨及物流相關
費用多及提列將金所致。 
資料來源：CMoney，國票投顧

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
下半年營收、獲利逐季向上 
AI 伺服器、車用及小訊號產品訂單能見度佳，強茂 B/B ratio 值維持在 1.3~1.5 高檔
位置，強茂 8 月營收 13.76 億元，MoM-4.89%，YoY+29.82%，3Q26 新增部份產
能，加上產品調漲，預期營收及毛利率持續向上，4Q26 有機會切入另一家 CSP 供應
鏈，年底增加封測產能(小訊號為主)、越南 TOREX 預計在 1Q27 交割，整體而言營運
動能無虞。預估強茂 3Q26 營收 42.51 億元，QoQ+7.47%，YoY+30.02%，毛利率
34.44%，稅後淨利 5.58 億元，QoQ+14.39%，YoY+84.08%，EPS 1.46 元。 
歐美重心轉移、非紅供應鏈，台 IDM 廠有訂價權 
在 AI 需求強勁推動下 ， 單機櫃功耗持續上升並導入高壓直流電架構 ， 帶動功率元件量
價同步提升，應用於伺服器功率元件年複合成長率遠高於產業平均，在產能吃緊情況
下，歐美 IDM 廠將重心轉向利潤較佳的高壓、SiC 以及低壓大電流的 DrMOS 核心市
場，排擠中低壓功率元件產能。同時，安世、揚傑等中資背景企業面臨去中化，伺服
器、車用工控等領域加速將訂單轉向台廠。 
目前成熟製程與後段封裝產能極度吃緊，具備晶圓代工、晶片設計到封裝完整布局的 
強茂等 IDM 廠，憑藉產品線的廣度及垂直整合優勢，能確保供貨不中斷並免受外包
產能排擠 ， 更實質掌握了稀缺產能的訂價權 ， 在這一波由AI 架構升級與地緣政治雙軌
驅動的轉單潮中，將成為優先且最大受惠者。 
機櫃功率愈大，功率元件量價同步提升 
AI 應用佔強茂營收比重 11%，多數來自運算托盤(compute tray)上 Power MOSFET、
Schottky、TVS 或小訊號等功率產品，運算托盤上功率元件產值佔整機櫃超過一半，
主要核心在整合式供電模組(DrMOS)，DrMOS 技術門檻高，由歐美大廠主導，台廠
不易切入，但供電相數的增加，將同步提升對周邊功率元件的需求量。 
以 GB300 為例，單顆 GPU 功率 1.4kW，電壓 0.8V，核心負載輸出電流是 1,750A，
根據單顆高密度 DrMOS / SPS 晶片的最大承載電流(70A~130A)規格去設計供電相
數(Power Phase) ，每一相數都必須配置一套完整的硬體元件，當 GPU 功率提升至
2.3kW (Vera Rubin) ，GPU 功率增加 60%，供電相數及功率元件需求量亦將等幅放
大。 強茂功率元件已成功打入CSP 大廠 ， 未來將持續受惠運算托盤上功率元件量的提
升及質的改變(如 TVS 相對大電流，晶粒面積變大，單價提升)，產業成長趨勢明確。 
預估強茂 2027 年 EPS 6.95 元，建議買進，目標價 208 元 
投研部預估強茂 2026 年營收 159.39 億元，YoY+21.73%，稅後淨利 19.21 億元，
YoY+61.22%，EPS 5.03 元；預估 2027 年營收 183.38 億元，YoY+15.05%，稅後淨
利 26.57 億元，YoY+38.29%，EPS 6.95 元。目前強茂 PE 24X，2026 年最高本益比
已超越 2017~2018 年、2020~2022 年功率元件景氣循環 20X~30X 區間，AI 伺服
器的應用有助拉升功率元件產業及公司評價， 給予強茂買進 建議，目標價 208 元
(2027F PE 30X)。

Document 063: 260918_daiwa_ Jentech

Source PDF: all report pdf/260918_daiwa_ Jentech.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-18
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We received an update from Jentech on 18 September. Its 
share price has been negatively impacted week to date by market talk 
concerning the adoption of removable lids in Vera Rubin and Rubin Ultra. 
However, we maintain our positive view on its ongoing product-upgrade 
roadmap and expect the ASP-upgrade trend to continue in the following 
years. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our latest memo, Record-high 
monthly revenue for August; 3Q26 QTD revenue tracking ahead, on 7 
September 2026).

What’s the impact: Promising ASP upgrade trend. Due to rising thermal 
design power (TDP), we expect the ASP of Jentech’s removable lid for 
Vera Rubin or Rubin Ultra to be 5-6x higher vs. that of the current one-
piece lid. Following better-than-expected shipments during the ramp-up of 
Vera Rubin, we now forecast its 3Q26 revenue at TWD9.65bn (previously 
TWD9.26bn vs. Bloomberg consensus of TWD9.62bn) and 4Q26 revenue 
at TWD10.62bn (previously TWD10.20bn, vs. Bloomberg consensus of 
TWD10.32bn). We believe there could be some revenue upside potential, 
subject to the adoption of removable lids for Vera Rubin or Rubin Ultra. We 
now forecast an EPS CAGR of 86.1% over 2026-28E, from 78.8% 
previously, as we believe further product upgrades and a favourable 
product mix should continue to support improving profitability.

Bright outlook for GPU projects as well as ASIC projects. We believe 
Jentech has a more promising product roadmap for ASIC clients, with 
potential ASP upgrades from the current stiffener. After GPU clients adopt 
removable lids, it also could encourage its ASIC clients to adopt removable 
lids, translating to 15-20x ASP increase from here. We raise our 2026-28E 
EPS by 6-15%, mainly as a result of our more aggressive revenue and 
gross-/operating-margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reiterate our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD9,420 (from TWD6,770), still based on a target PER of 73x (vs. its 
past-3-year PER range of 17-62x) applied to our 1-year forward EPS 
forecast, which is rolled over to 4Q26E-3Q27E (from 3Q26E-2Q27E). We 
believe our target valuation is fair, given Jentech’s strong earnings visibility 
and leading position in chip-level thermal solutions, which we believe 
warrant a rerating in addition to its strong moat and competitive landscape 
in the segment. Downside risks: weaker-than-expected global AI server 
demand and slower-than-expected thermal spec upgrades.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 1-10% above Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more aggressive in our revenue and gross-
/operating-margin assumptions.

18 September 2026

3Q26 preview: stronger-than-expected spec upgrades

➢ We maintain our positive view on Jentech’s product roadmap 
➢ Likely EPS upward revision trend in the following months 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12-month TP to TWD9,420

Company profile 
Established in 1987, Jentech Precision Industrial is a leading global thermal-solutions and precision-
metal components maker that serves leading semiconductor and server vendors. As of 2025, its 
revenue comprised thermal heat spreaders (73%), lead frames (9%), electronic components (7%), 
communication connectors (1%) and others (10%).

ESG analysis

Update Date: 29 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
The CEO and chairperson of Jentech is the same individual, Mr. Zhao Zongxin. We note that Mr.
Zhao has extensive long-term experience in senior management within the company and across
its subsidiaries, which is a positive sign from an executive perspective.
Capital management 1
Between 2021-25, Jentech has maintained a dividend payout ratio of around 60%, which we
consider reasonable for balancing shareholder returns with reinvestment needs. This consistent
approach reflects prudent capital management discipline and a stable financial policy.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company engages in transactions with subsidiaries primarily for normal operational
purposes, such as purchase of materials and components. The proportion of these costs to
overall COGS remains low, and no irregular or non-transparent transactions were identified.
E Supply chain
management 1
Jentech has a structured procurement process, including supplier evaluation, signature of
procurement contracts, and requirements for compliance with environmental and safety
regulations. The company also diversifies sourcing channels (major suppliers from Taiwan,
Japan, Europe, Korea, USA) and maintains stable supply relationships, with clear
communication protocols for prompt response to supplier issues. This reflects strong supply
chain transparency and robust quality control measures.
E Data security 1
The company has established an information security team, conducts annual internal control
audits, and implements procedures such as asset inventory, network security monitoring, virus
prevention systems, firewall management, and access rights control. Backup systems and
disaster recovery mechanisms are in place, allowing the company to restore information system
operations and minimize damage during major incidents or equipment failures.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

TSMC: AI growth broadens beyond GPU/ ASIC  — TSMC remains central to nearly
every major AI hardware transition. The most important point is that its AI
opportunity is broader than the GPU/ASIC revenue definition investors typically
focus on. Networking silicon, AI CPUs, silicon photonics and advanced packaging
provide incremental growth vectors, while the underlying compute roadmap
continues migrating rapidly toward leading-edge nodes. We expect stronger 2027

growth for TSMC thanks to full-year contribution from 2nm, increased 3nm AI-
chip volumes and potential pricing increases, creating another powerful
combination of volume, mix and ASP expansion. In terms of competitive
landscape, we think Intel still needs substantial leading-edge capacity for its own
CPUs and appears constrained in its ability to simultaneously support internal
products and large external AI customers. In addition, Intel’s 14A ecosystem and

PDK/customer engagement process would require considerable qualification and
design-cycle time before translating into meaningful external production. For
critical AI products, customers also need predictable multiyear capacity
commitments; TSMC’s manufacturing scale and supply-chain coordination remain
important competitive advantages. Nevertheless, we see potential opportunities
for Intel and UMC’s collaboration at 12nm, that could support products such as

connectivity, display-driver or consumer-related chips, while its advanced
packaging technology could also win specialized programs. However, those
opportunities should be distinguished from a broad challenge to TSMC’s leading-
edge foundry position. Strategically, we think TSMC is becoming more selective
about where it deploys capital and engineering resources. We believe front-end
wafer manufacturing, SoIC and differentiated technologies such as silicon

photonics remain priorities, while more conventional back-end operations are
increasingly suitable for outsourcing. This suggests that continued AI growth can
benefit TSMC directly through leading-edge wafer revenue while simultaneously
creating substantial opportunities for OSAT partners.
TSMC outsourcing creates a structural opportunity for ASEH- Given the tight
supply for both advanced node wafer and CoWoS, we think leading-edge front-
end manufacturing generates the highest strategic value, and we believe TSMC has

an incentive to prioritize wafer fabrication and differentiated packaging
technologies such as SoIC while outsourcing larger portions of more back-end
processing. CoWoS capacity can continue expanding, but TSMC’s own back-end
investment intensity may eventually moderate as qualified OSAT partners assume
more steps. ASE/SPIL are already participating in substrate-related outsourced
work and should gain additional opportunities as the ecosystem matures. Other

Taiwanese packaging companies can also participate, while interposer production
itself could become more distributed across external partners. Importantly, ASEH
is not dependent solely on TSMC outsourcing. It is developing its own full-process
advanced-packaging capabilities, including FoCoS, providing a more direct route
to capture high-value AI packaging revenue. AMD is an important
customer/catalyst for ASEH, in our view, particularly as CPU and heterogeneous-

compute architectures require increasingly sophisticated packaging. ASEH’s
management’s expectation that leading-edge advanced-packaging revenue can
roughly double underscores the potential operating leverage. As chiplets, HBM,
silicon photonics and increasingly complex interconnects proliferate, packaging
content per device rises and the technical barriers to entry increase, potentially
supporting structurally better margins for ASEH.
CPO is emerging as the next major AI infrastructure transition - Co-packaged

optics (CPO) appears to be one of the clearest structural transitions in AI hardware
because it addresses two increasingly severe constraints simultaneously: data
transmission bandwidth and power consumption. We expect Nvidia should be the
initial catalyst, first through Spectrum-X CPO switches and subsequently
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 3

Taiwan Semiconductors
21 September 2026 Citi Research
3
through CPO integration associated with next-generation NVLink scale-up
architectures and Rubin Ultra. Importantly, there are two different CPO adoption
timelines that are often confused. CPO embedded directly alongside GPU/compute
packages could remain two to three years away, but switch-level CPO is
considerably closer and should begin scaling earlier. Moving optical connectivity
closer to the switching silicon can materially reduce electrical I/O distance and

power while supporting higher aggregate bandwidth. During Semicon Taiwan in
early Sep, CPO coupling, optical alignment, fiber-array assembly and testing were
among the most prominent themes. TSMC is developing its COUPE silicon-
photonics/packaging platform, while a broader ecosystem is forming around
optical engines, lasers, fiber-array units (FAUs), receptacles, precision alignment
and test equipment. The most visible near-term bottlenecks appear to be external

laser sources and fiber/FAU capacity, helping explain aggressive expansion by
suppliers such as Lumentum and Corning. The transition could also redistribute
semiconductor supply-chain value as CPO moves more value into advanced
semiconductor packaging, silicon photonics, optical coupling and testing. We
believe Largan’s high precision automation capability will be able to catch CPO
opportunity particularly in the most challenging optical fiber array alignment

process.
Custom ASIC: MediaTek and GUC riding with Google - The Google TPU ecosystem
illustrates both the opportunity and the execution complexity of custom AI silicon.
We believe MediaTek’s role should increase substantially over coming generations,
MediaTek’ s target market share is 15–20% initially but we see more upside ahead.
With current TPU v8 generation, MediaTek’s responsibility is concentrated more
heavily in I/O, SerDes and wafer-handling-related activities; in the subsequent

generation, its scope expands toward portions of the compute implementation as
well. The SerDes configuration itself is also expected to increase, while advanced-
node compute content materially lifts MediaTek’s dollar value per device.
Consequently, revenue can grow much faster than TPU units or market share alone
would suggest. One execution risk is advanced packaging. A future
Google/MediaTek program is expected to utilize Intel’s EMIB-T technology, which is

less proven at very high volume than TSMC’s mature CoWoS ecosystem. Much of
the difficult integration work, however, is distributed across substrate suppliers
and OSAT partners. We think the initial yields and volumes could therefore be
constrained, but the program has strategic importance for Google, Intel and
MediaTek and is likely to proceed despite an early learning curve. And we believe its
existing TPU generations should also continue shipping during the transition,

cushioning the revenue impact. We believe Google’s broader engagement with
AMD and Marvell should not automatically be interpreted as additional TPU
suppliers, in our view, Marvell has important networking and storage relationships
with Google, while AMD opportunities appear more closely associated with CPUs.
More broadly, the ASIC ecosystem remains structurally more fragmented than
Nvidia’s platform. Google is gradually internalizing more silicon expertise, but it still

requires external design-service and implementation partners. We also see
substantial growth of Google’s CPU at GUC, which is fully COT model with back-
end only service.
AI systems and power: Hon Hai benefits from Rubin, while 800VDC adoption
should be gradual- At the system level, Hon Hai remains a major beneficiary of
Nvidia’s rack-scale transition. This year’s growth has been driven largely by higher
AI rack shipments, but next year adds a much higher ASP as the supply chain

transitions from GB300 toward Vera Rubin. Higher rack value means revenue and
profit can rise substantially even if unit growth moderates, provided margins
remain broadly stable. Beyond Nvidia, Hon Hai is also positioning itself to
manufacture custom-ASIC and hyperscaler systems, including CPU- and TPU-
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Taiwan Semiconductors
21 September 2026 Citi Research
4
related racks, which could begin contributing more meaningfully later next year.
Power architecture is another major debate, particularly around 800VDC. The
key distinction is that 800VDC is strategically attractive but not necessarily
mandatory for Rubin or even every Rubin Ultra deployment. Rack power rises
materially from the GB300 generation toward Rubin, requiring changes such as
sidecar power shelves and more sophisticated power distribution. New greenfield

AI data centers and neocloud deployments have stronger incentives to design
around 800VDC because they can optimize the entire facility for future high-
density racks. Existing facilities, however, can continue using alternative
architectures, making adoption gradual rather than immediate. We believe Delta
remains structurally well positioned in high-power data-center infrastructure, but
expectations around the speed of 800VDC adoption may be slower than expected.

The industry also faces a significant engineering learning curve as rack power
density rises, including thermal management and potential overheating issues.
Separately, shortages of power-management ICs and capacitors remain important
constraints regardless of whether a data center uses 800VDC. Analog and power-
semiconductor supply is supported by both AI-driven demand and relatively tight
capacity at IDM suppliers and specialty foundries such as UMC and VIS.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
CCL –  Doosan announces
capacity expansion in Korea and
China
What’s new? According to Business Korea, Doosan (000150.KS, not covered)
announced a KRW970B CCL capacity expansion on Sep 17:
• KRW420B in Korea to add optical module CCL lines, and
• KRW550B in China to build a new plant for AI accelerator and network
switch CCL.
Spending is phased through 2028, with the new lines coming online sequentially

from 2H28.
Our thoughts:  Our checks suggest this investment could add ~2,000K sheets per
month of CCL capacity at Doosan, equivalent to ~2% of global CCL capacity in 2028,
on our estimates. We view the investment as further evidence of robust demand for
high-end CCL and do not believe it undermines our thesis of continued high-end CCL
scarcity (see our CCL initiation report). As such, we see a positive read-across for
EMC, TUC, and Shengyi. We remain OW on EMC and TUC, while maintaining our EW

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
September 20, 2026 07:52 PM GMT

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 2

Hon Precision (7769.TW)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Hon Precision
Valuation
Our target price of NT$11,000 is based on a target PER multiple of 36x applied to the average of our 2027E/28E EPS estimates.
Our PER target of 35x is set at 1-std above its 1-year PER average, justified by its high share of the AI/HPC testing handler
market, which we estimate could support an earnings CAGR of 77% in 2025-28, higher than its 3-year CAGR at c40% in 2022-

25. Our estimated 77% earning CAGR is also higher than the +40% average (Bloomberg consensus) of its global testing
equipment peers. As its peers have been trading at  average  36x PER on 2027/28 consensus EPS in the past 1 year, we believe
Hon Precision deserves a further re-rating to our target PER of 36x.
Risks
Our quantitative risk rating system, which looks at historical share price volatility, rates Hon Precision High Risk. However, we
are not assigning a High Risk rating as we forecast an earnings CAGR of >75%, significantly stronger than the growth outlook

for its global peers. Also, we note its dominant position in AI/HPC handler market and expanding market TAM opportunity,
which support our constructive view over its growth outlook.
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target price: 1) Global AI demand slowdown  driving weaker
capacity build for semiconductor equipment; 2) unexpected share losses at its major AI projects; 3) slower-than-expected
capacity ramp at its new facilities, or the company taking a long time to find/secure new sites; 4) slower-than-expected

development of its CPO business; 5) shorter-than-expected testing time leading to lower demand for its products; 6) weak
adoption of high-premium cold plate products; and 7) increasing competition from new entrants.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

TFT-LCD Panel Prices for Sep-
26: TVs, Monitors and NBs Flat
MoM
Chinese panel makers are planning to raise panel prices in the
coming months to mitigate the cost pressure, which we believe
could find some resistance among their brand customers. Glass
opportunities in advanced packaging remain the key reasons that
major panel makers are trading at 1.5-2.0x P/B.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

75" 4K 65" 4K 55" FHD 50" FHD 43" FHD 32" HD
Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices stayed flat MoM in September: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and
75" TV panel prices all stood flat for the month of September,  in line with our
expectations. While the overall TV panel demand was lackluster, there is still some
seasonal demand as brands are preparing for year-end promotions. Recently, the
major Chinese panel makers have notified their customers of price hikes from

September/October in order to mitigate increasing margin pressure caused by rising
costs of various components, including driver ICs, glass substrates and PCB.
Although major Chinese panel makers might leverage their annual maintenance of
three to seven days during October 2026 as a bargaining chip, we think it might take
more negotiations with brand customers before they can really raise panel prices,
since order momentum will slow down in the coming months. On a quarterly

average basis, TV panel prices  declined in the low-single-digits % QoQ in 3Q26.
IT panel prices were flat MoM in  September: Monitor and notebook panel prices
stayed flat MoM during the month. Both applications continues to  see slower
demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about  increasing
component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and reluctant to
given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect IT panel

prices to remain flat QoQ in 3Q26.
Risk/reward less attractive: We think that the current valuation of Taiwanese panel
makers, AUO (2409.TW) and Innolux (3481.TW) both at 1.7x P/B, has already baked
in some expectations of glass in advanced packaging for revenue contribution in
2028. However, some supply chain players think this might take longer, and there
seem to be more suppliers from different supply chains vying for this opportunity. In
China, while expectations are also building for the glass core substrate, we have a

relative preference for BOE (000725.SZ; 1.5x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ; 1.8x P/B),
on its scale advantage and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Current valuation of 1.5-2.0x P/B have already priced in the expectation of glass
in advanced packaging from 2H28.
Nonetheless,  the risk in commercialization and competition are worth monitoring.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trending Down from 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM, August was down 1% MoM, and September was flat MoM,   in line
with our expectation that TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That
said, we do not expect a significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide

downside support.

Below-seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization  at ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance

following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.
Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, followed by a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing

structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to
profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural
Changes on Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In, June 17, 2021).

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Fair After Recent Correction
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply-chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.6x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.8x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end demand: If end demand for major applications turns out to be better
than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

next generation AI server rack ramp up on growing AI spending, which also leads to a 
lower blended GM considering the lower GM of AI server racks compared to other

Valuation: We continue to use near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP . Our target PE 
multiple is updated to 10.7x 2027E EPS (vs. 11.0x previously), which is derived from the 
refreshed peers’ correlation between forward trading P/E and earnings growth. With our 
updated earnings estimates and target multiple, our 12M TP is unchanged at NT$299. 
Our target P/E is still between the company’s historical avg. and avg.-1stv. trading P/E. 
Maintain Neutral.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral-rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$299 is based on an 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 21 Sep 2026 close.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What’s new? ITEQ (6213.TW, not covered) reported August 2026 profit per TWSE
regulatory requirements:
• August revenue was NT$5,044M (+6% m/m, +98% y/y). July/August
revenues accounted for 67% of the consensus 3Q revenue estimate.
• August pre-tax profit was NT$1,302M (+762% y/y), 52% of the consensus 3Q
forecast.
• August net profit was NT$924M (+853% y/y), 55% of the consensus 3Q

estimate.
Our thoughts: ITEQ is a leading CCL supplier, with infrastructure applications
(servers, switches, telecom, etc.) accounting for ~73% of 2Q26 revenue. We view its
August earnings as a positive read-through for EMC, TUC and KB Laminates within
our coverage. The margin improvement is consistent with effective cost pass-
through and potentially wider price-cost spreads, which could continue to support
further profitability expansion going forward. We remain OW on all three names.

Key Takeaways
August pre-tax and net margins reached 25.8% and 18.3%, respectively, vs. 15.9%
and 11.1% in 2Q26.
With July/August revenue broadly tracking quarterly consensus, we think
August’s outsized earnings contribution points to upside risk to 3Q profit
expectations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Pegatron (4938.TW): AI servers in expansion; New smartphone model 
with Broadband entering peak season to support growth ahead; Sell
28 September 2026 | 10:33AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, 
mainly driven by AI server racks ramp up with higher ASP and major smartphone 
brand customer’s new model launches (report link). Overall, we project Pegatron’s 
3Q/4Q26E revenues to grow +18%/+25% YoY, driven by: (1) new model launches 
from a major smartphone customer supporting September and 4Q26 revenue 
growth; (2) a continuing ramp-up in AI server shipments, across rack-level and 
baseboard-based, riding on the growing demand in Neocloud and enterprises, (3) 
broadband, IoT, and game console entering peak season. We are positive on 
Pegatron’s expansion from consumer electronics to AI servers and automotive 
electronics, while the high memory costs could cap PC and smartphone market 
growth. Maintain Sell on stretched valuation.

Pegatron’s Aug revenue was up 34% YoY / 3% MoM to NT$88bn, or 12% lower than 
our estimate. This reflects that high memory costs continue to cap PC and 
smartphone growth, despite a strong ramp-up in AI servers. Aug PC shipments was 
down 49% YoY to 0.40-0.45m, reflecting the impact of consumption pull in under 
the high memory costs. Its major smartphone brand customer launched new models 
in Sep, supporting Pegatron’s growth in Sep and 4Q26; prior to September, the 
company suffered from model transition (i.e. declining demand for older models 
while new models were in a slow shipment ramp-up phase).

### Page 2

Earnings revision: We factor in Pegatron’s Aug revenue and our 2026-28E net income 
largely unchanged. We cut our 2026-28E revenues by 1% / 1% / 1% to reflect the miss in 
consumer electronics under high memory costs. Our 2026-28E GM, OPM and NM are 
largely unchanged.

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12M TP based on near-term P/E, and our target 
P/E is updated to 10.8x 2027E EPS, (vs. 10.6x previously), derived from the refreshed 
peers correlation of forward year earnings growth and P/E.  With the updated earnings 
and target multiple, our 12M TP is revised up to NT$76.9 (vs. NT$75.5 previously). The 
target P/E of 10.8x is within the company’s historical avg. and avg.-1std forward trading 
P/E. Maintain Sell.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Pegatron

faster-than-expected AI server ramp-up, and 3) stronger-than-expected demand on 
consumer electronics.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

ab 28 September 2026
Global Research
UBS Global I/O Semiconductors
Foundry: Much larger N2 TAM and faster A14
expansion; mature foundry upcycle underway
N2 demand inflection larger than expected
Our proprietary bottoms-up N2 foundry TAM analysis points to substantially larger
demand of 320kwpm by 2028E, versus our prior estimate of approximately 230kwpm a
year ago. The increase is driven primarily by stronger server CPU demand and
accelerating AI compute deployments, particularly as a growing number of ASIC

programs ramp into volume production alongside GPUs over the next several years. At
the same time, we  expect Intel to migrate a larger portion of its PC CPU production to
18A as execution improves. As a result, we estimate server CPUs and accelerators will
account for 54% of N2 foundry demand by 2028, up from our previous estimate of
44%, while PCs should contribute 19% and smartphones/tablets 26%. (See Figure 6Q u a n t if y in g  N 2  d e m a n d  o p p o r t u n it ie s  f o r  f o u n d r y  -

Figure 7N 2  f o u n d r y  d e m a n d  b y  a p p lic a t io n  (2 0 2 8 E )). The larger N2 opportunity supports robust revenue growth for TSMC through
2028-29E as the industry's major foundry supplier, even as competition from Samsung
Foundry, Intel, and Terafab increases modestly.
Further acceleration of N3/2 expansion; quicker A14 capacity build-up
To meet the larger N2 demand outlook, we raise our estimate for industry N2 capacity at
end-2028 to 312kwpm, up from 230kwpm previously. We forecast TSMC's N2 capacity

reaching 210kwpm in 2028, versus our prior estimate of 160kwpm. Intel should also
scale capacity more aggressively, exceeding 60kwpm in 2028E for internal CPU
production, compared with our previous forecast of 40kwpm. Meanwhile,
strengthening cloud AI demand in 2027-28E is pushing our industry N3 capacity
forecast to 290-300kwpm by 2028, up from 270kwpm previously, driven entirely by
faster expansion at TSMC. TSMC's A14 node remains on track for mass production in

2028, supported by steady R&D progress and growing customer engagement. Industry
feedback suggests TSMC is pulling forward its expansion schedule, targeting
approximately 60kwpm of A14 capacity in 2028 when the node enters mass
production, based on  UBSe. Reflecting the more aggressive leading-edge expansion, we
raise our TSMC capex forecast to US$90bn in 2027 and US$105bn in 2028, up from US
$63bn this year. See our Q326 earnings preview.
Mature foundry: favorable supply/demand and pricing outlook in 2027-28

We were early in advocating the anti-consensus view of a mature foundry upcycle,
driven by an industry-wide supply and resource reallocation. Utilization and pricing
trends have continued to improve throughout 2026, and we expect more meaningful
earnings upside in 2027-28. Our updated analysis suggests 8-inch foundry utilization
could rise to 95% in 2027, up from 85% in 2026, supported by growing server PMIC
demand. While 12-inch 28/40nm demand may face near-term headwinds from weaker

smartphone and consumer demand, we still expect utilization to improve to 88% in
2027 from 84% in 2026, supported by modest capacity growth. We also believe
competitive behaviour from Chinese foundries should remain rational into 2027, as
major players increasingly prioritize capital allocation toward leading-edge technologies.
Stock recommendations
We reiterate Buy on TSMC and expect further upward revisions to Street revenue, EPS
and capex estimates for 2027-28. Within mature foundry, UMC remains our top pick, as

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
The semiconductor industry is cyclically sensitive to the economy and so upside and downside
risks relate to macro drivers. Other, semiconductor capital equipment specific downside risks
include the rapid loss of market share due to product timing/performance , design or
distribution. The supply chain companies into the handset industry also operate in a highly
cyclical market both from macro concerns and inventory cycles within the supply chain. These

companies also operate in a highly competitive environment with product innovation and
R&D investments critical to success.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

such as structure, management, performance record, discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-28

Status: ok

### Page 1

ab 28 September 2026
Global Research
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing
Q326 earnings preview: Raising 2027-28E capex
for next phase of AI growth
Cloud AI mega-trend driving sales growth >40% in 2026E and 40% in 2027E
Feedback from our recent industry analysis suggests no signs of Cloud AI demand
moderation (Summit Day 1, Day 2&3, CoWoS update); yet the demand outlook has
further strengthened across CPUs, GPUs and ASICs in 2027-28. TSMC is on track to

reach 2026 sales guidance of slightly over 40% growth in USD (UBSe 41%), and our
growth analysis points to sustained 40% sales growth in 2027E, above consensus of
36%. We estimate Q3 sales at 14% QoQ in USD and GM at 67.1%, both at high-end of
guidance. We forecast Q4 sales to grow 10% QoQ thanks to seasonal build of flagship
phones and accelerating ramp of high performance compute (HPC), with flat GM of
67.0% considering the dilution of N2 and overseas expansion.

Further step-up of capex in 2027-28E
We have been constructive about TSMC's capex and view higher capex essential in
solidifying TSMC's leadership in advanced foundry. We keep 2026E capex at US$63bn,
and raise 2027/28E capex to US$90bn/105bn from US$80bn/95bn previously. Higher
estimates are based on: 1) our industry analysis shows that TSMC is further accelerating
expansion across N3/2 and  pulls forward tool delivery schedule of A14 for larger capacity

by 2028 when in mass production; 2) more meaningful price inflation of equipment,
and required prepayment; 3) higher capex for advanced packaging. We forecast capital
intensity to rise moderately from 36.6% in 2026 to 37.4% in 2027 but rnomalize to
32.6% in 2028. We lift TSMC's N3/2 capacity estimates at end of 2028 to
220/210kwpm, vs 200/160kwpm prior. See our foundry sector note for details.
What will the earnings call focus on?
1) Overall semi cycle outlook. UBS forecasts semis (ex memory) revenue to grow

31%/15% in 2026/27 led by Cloud AI. UBS remains conservative on consumer end
demand, estimating further 3%/4% declines for smartphone/PC units in 2027. 2)
TSMC's capex strategy over the next 3-4 years,  and whether any bottlenecks could
constrain its expansion plans. 3) Competition, especially as peers such as Samsung
Foundry and Terafab have shown increased ability to invest, although we also believe
TSMC may engage for potential angles to collaborate on Terafab. 4) Gross margin in

H226/2027. We forecast GM to decline slightly  to 67.0% in H226, and stabilize at
67.2% in 2027, underpinned by price hike, scale benefits and improved product mix.
Valuation: reiterate Buy and target of NT$3,650
We raise 2027E and beyond capex  and lift long-term earnings CAGR from 25% to 27%.
Our target of NT$3,650 is based on 21x 2027-28E PE. We see meaningful re-rating
potential from the current 16.6x/12.2x 2027/28E PE . Key catalysts include stronger

2027-28E growth outlook, continued TAM expansion and higher capex spend.

Company Description
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing (TSMC) is the world's leading dedicated foundry
service provider, with annual capacity of about 32m of 8-inch equivalent wafers as of end-
2024. It operates four 12-inch wafer fabs, four 8-inch wafer fabs and one 6-inch wafer fab in

Taiwan. TSMC also manages two 8-inch fabs and one 12-inch fab overseas under wholly
owned subsidiaries WaferTech in the US, and TSMC China and TSMC Nanjing in China.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target for TSMC on a PE multiple.
We think TSMC faces a variety of risks, including rapidly changing technology, intense
competition, high capital investment and cyclical demand. TSMC also has a history of paying
stock options to its employees, which could dilute earnings.

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

旺季獲利上修，淡季運價有撐

永豐觀點 
亞洲港口擁擠、船舶繞航、巴拿馬運河水位低下降低供給周轉支撐淡季運價有撐。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：(1) 3Q 美洲線運價優於預期旺季獲利佳，巴拿馬運河水位低下支撐 4Q
運價相對高檔，(2) 亞洲線進入旺季相對暢旺，(3) 歷史 PBR 落在 0.5-4.3 倍，目
前 PBR 為 0.54 倍 ， 評價處於相對低檔 ， 維持買進 ， 目標價由71 元(0.7X2026BVPS)
調整到 73 元(0.65 X2027 BVPS)。

陽明為全球第九大海運業者：陽明海運是全球第九大航商，2026/9 運力 77.3 萬
TEU 約 99 艘(較 2025 年底的 71.4 萬 TEU 成長 8.3%)，市佔率 2.2%，加入 THE 
Alliance 聯盟 (聯盟成 員之 一赫伯羅特2025/1 退出 ，聯盟更名為 Premier 
Alliance(2025/2-2030/1)。安裝脫硫塔比重超過 6 成，自有櫃佔 54%。1H26 年
美洲線佔營收比重 36%，歐洲線佔 39%，近洋佔 13%，其他航線(中東、南美等)
佔 12%，運送量 223.6 萬 TEU(+4%YoY)，平均運價 1012 美元/TEU(-5%YoY)。
股權方面，官股持有 31%。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 為 5.37 元：受惠美洲線與亞洲線運價上漲，加上 LNG 船投
入地中海航線 ， 預估營收656 億元(+56%YoY)， 稅後獲利187.5 億元(+210%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 為 5.37 元。4Q 歐洲線進入淡季、美洲線受巴拿馬運河水位降低相對有
撐、亞洲進入旺季加上塞港相對強勢，公司認為4Q 呈現緩步修正，研究部預估營
收 573 億元(+53%YoY)，稅後獲利 136.4 億元(+496%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 3.91
元。 
燃油成本的影響：陽明 1H26 年燃油成本佔 16%，6 月油價攀升至 692 美元/噸，
整體燃油成本較去年同期+1%。2Q 高硫油平均 617 美元/噸(+23.5%QoQ)，低硫
油平均 775 美元/噸(+24.5%QoQ)，因地緣政治影響，3Q 高硫油平均 562 美元/
噸-9%QoQ)，低硫油平均 792 美元/噸(+2.3%QoQ)，研究部假設陽明年耗油量
142 萬噸，船用燃油平均一年漲 100 美元/噸，燃油成本增加 44 億元，EPS 影響
1.26 元，不過航商透過燃油附加費的徵收以分散燃油成本上漲的壓力。

2026 年供給成長是近幾年最低一年：巴拿馬運河當局 9/4 將每天通行艘樹從 36
艘降至 34 艘，9/15 再降到 32 艘，目前運河水位較去年低 2.5%，預期 10 月水位
將低於去年同期 3.8%，艙位供給將再度限縮。無論是 Drewry，Clarkson 或是
Alphaliner 預估 2026 年船舶供給成長率是近幾年最低的 ， 加上紅海改道的船舶回
歸延遲，有效運力釋放再度放緩，2026 年是供給相對較小的一年。 
3Q26 第 2 艘雙燃料船交船：新船在手訂單 23 艘約 30 萬 TEU，1Q26-4Q26 年
將交 5 艘 1.56 萬 TEU LNG 船(已交 4 艘)，3Q28-2Q29 交 7 艘 1.6 萬 TEU LNG
雙燃料船，4Q28-1Q30 交 6 艘 8 千 TEU 甲醇雙燃料船，4Q28-3Q29 年交 6 艘
1.3 萬 TEU LNG 雙燃料船。轉投資光明海運則預計 3Q28-2Q29 交 4 艘 6.4 萬
DWT 散裝船。

欣興 (3037 TT) 
ABF 漲幅優於預期，獲利再度上修

永豐觀點 
3Q26 欣興 ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點，中線T-glass 玻纖布於 3Q27 前
供給吃緊，長線 ABF 供不應求至 2030 年，載板報價可望逐季推升 5-15%，獲利
持續高速成長，近期股價下跌反應檢調搜索利空，評價已修正至合理區間。 
 投資評價與建議 
欣興受惠 ABF 帶動產品組合轉佳，長線而言 ABF 於 2030 年前供給吃緊，載板報
價漲勢由成本推升改為需求拉動，3Q26 漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點，且本波循環
公司通吃 CoWoS 及 EMIB-T 訂單，客戶遍及 GPU、ASIC 及 CPU 訂單，技術能
力僅次於龍頭廠 Ibiden， 研究部再度上調2026-2028 年稅後 EPS 至 23.57、42.71
及 65.08 元，投資建議買進，考量檢調風波對評價承壓，目標價維持 1575 元
(24X2028 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
欣興企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2023 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

欣興為國內 ABF 龍頭廠商：欣興為國內專業 IC 載板製造商，全球市佔率 18%僅
次於 Ibiden 以及 Samsung，2Q26 產品組合為 ABF(52%)、BT(9%)、HDI(27%)、
PCB(9%)、FPC(2%)及其它(1%)，其中 IC 載板毛利率優於公司平均水準。下游客
戶包含 Nokia、APPLE、Nvidia 以及 HTC，主要競爭對手為 Ibiden、Samsung、
Shinko、南電以及景碩。 
3Q26 營收達成率 69.6%， 暗示ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 ：欣興 8 月營收來到 177.46
億元(+9.2%MoM，+56.3%YoY)，除延續自 3 月以來月月創高紀錄外值得留意的
是月增及年增幅度亦同步擴大，整體 3Q26 營收達成率 69.6%亦優於原預期，暗
示 ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點以上，研究部上調3Q26 營收至 525.83 億
元(+22.6%QoQ，+54.7%YoY)， 毛利率25.8%， 稅後淨利93.36 億元(-28.8%QoQ，
+325.4%YoY)，稅後 EPS 5.68 元(原估 5.26 元)。 
CPU 生命週期展開使 ABF 需求更具延續性：過去 ABF 需求主要聚焦 AI Server 需
求，研究部原預估 2024-2025 年 ABF 供過於求 2%，2026 年亦難見反轉。受惠
AI Server 於 GPU 及 ASIC 需求帶動，另一方面 general server 回溫有望使 ABF
需求由高階產品延續至中階產品，Ibiden 預估相關高階 ABF 需求高速成長，加計
Production load 換算 2028 年面積需求較 2024 年增加 10.7 倍 ， 相較AI/ASIC 產
能於 2028 年擴充 1.8 倍供給仍低於需求。 
檢調搜索風波影響營運有限：日前欣興合江廠遭檢調搜索，股價亦應聲下跌，研究
部認為欣興合江廠位於中壢，主要生產 PCB 產品 2Q26 營收占比 9%，雖然後續
可能因產地標示不明影響 PCB 客戶關係 ， 惟與目前主力營運ABF 載板以及 HDI 並
無關聯。ABF 在 AI Server 需求推動下 2030 年以前供不應求，目前高階 GPU、
ASIC 產品僅 Ibiden(4062 JP)及欣興(3037 TT)具備相關供應能力，自研 CPU 則有
景碩(3189 TT) 緊追在後，考量產品認證需時 6-8 個月、關鍵壓合機台 lead time
亦 1-1.5 年，短期轉單不易。 
ABF 報價優於預期，上調 2026-2028 年營運：欣興 2026 年資本支出 537 億元，
其中 85%用於 ABF 除搭配客戶陸續啟動光復及楊梅 2 廠擴產，楊梅 3 廠可望於
4Q26-1Q27 動土，以土建時程 1.5-2 年推算 2029 年可望加入營運行列，未來 2
年資本支出亦可望維持 400 億以上水準。研究部考量 ABF 整體供給吃緊態勢延續
以及公司通吃 CoWoS 及 EMIB-T 產品，先進封裝客戶群布局廣泛，研究部上調
